**[00:26:10.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000270)  
para ipakita ang aking

**[00:26:12.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000271)  
full support sa

**[00:26:14.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000272)  
budget ng DOLE.

**[00:26:16.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000273)  
Hindi po ako kontra. As a matter of fact,

**[00:26:18.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000274)  
papayag ako na madagdagan pa

**[00:26:20.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000275)  
because time and time again,

**[00:26:22.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000276)  
palagi ko pong

**[00:26:23.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000277)

**[00:26:25.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000278)  
yung kakulangan

**[00:26:28.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000279)  
sa personnel. Pagdating sa

**[00:26:30.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000280)

**[00:26:32.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000281)  
in-inspect yung mga

**[00:26:34.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000282)  
factory, mga

**[00:26:36.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000283)  
companies na hindi sumusunod

**[00:26:38.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000284)  
sa labor codes. Kaya

**[00:26:40.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000285)  
hindi sumusunod din sa

**[00:26:42.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000286)

**[00:26:45.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000287)  
Kaso na sabi sa akin,

**[00:26:46.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000288)  
kulang daw sa tao. Pag kulang

**[00:26:48.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000289)  
sa tao, hindi kulang sa budget.

**[00:26:50.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000290)  
At inaano ko nga,

**[00:26:53.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000291)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:26:54.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000292)

**[00:26:56.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000293)  
dapat ng body cam.

**[00:26:58.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000294)  
Ang lahat ng mga fields inspector.

**[00:27:00.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000295)  
Of course, that would also

**[00:27:02.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000296)

**[00:27:04.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000297)  
So, kung kailangan ng budget,

**[00:27:06.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000298)  
this is the time, sabihin nyo sa amin,

**[00:27:08.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000299)

**[00:27:10.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000300)

**[00:27:12.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000301)

**[00:27:15.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000302)  
Kasi Mr. Chair, very important yun eh.

**[00:27:17.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000303)  
Mga fields inspector pupunta doon

**[00:27:18.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000304)  
sa isang

**[00:27:20.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000305)

**[00:27:22.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000306)  
and then pagbalik,

**[00:27:24.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000307)  
hindi alam kung ano yung ginawang inspeksyon.

**[00:27:26.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000308)  
Usually, more often than not,

**[00:27:28.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000309)  
pupunta yung inspector doon

**[00:27:30.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000310)

**[00:27:32.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000311)  
mag-uusap yung HR sa ka-inspector,

**[00:27:34.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000312)

**[00:27:36.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000313)

**[00:27:39.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000314)  
So,

**[00:27:40.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000315)  
kawawa yung mga

**[00:27:41.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000316)  
nagreklamo mga manggagawa, hindi sila

**[00:27:43.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000317)

**[00:27:46.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000318)  
stuff like that. So, that is

**[00:27:48.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000319)  
very important

**[00:27:48.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000320)  
proper documentation para masiguro na

**[00:27:52.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000321)  
yung pagpunta doon ng

**[00:27:53.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000322)  
field inspector,

**[00:27:55.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000323)  
at maayos yung kanyang ginawang,

**[00:27:58.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000324)  
trabaho. And then, pangalawa,

**[00:28:00.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000325)  
Mr. Chair, yun sa NLRC,

**[00:28:01.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000326)  
isa po akong bumabatikos dati palagi sa

**[00:28:04.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000327)

**[00:28:05.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000328)  
lately nung pumunta ko

**[00:28:08.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000329)

**[00:28:09.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000330)

**[00:28:11.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000331)

**[00:28:14.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000332)

**[00:28:16.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000333)  
sa logistics, so they cannot,

**[00:28:18.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000334)  
we cannot expect so much from them

**[00:28:20.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000335)  
if we don't support them

**[00:28:22.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000336)  
in terms of budget.

**[00:28:23.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000337)  
At meron ako na balitaan

**[00:28:25.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000338)  
na merong bagong building sana,

**[00:28:27.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000339)  
pinapatayo yung NLRC.

**[00:28:28.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000340)  
At umabot na nang

**[00:28:30.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000341)

**[00:28:32.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000342)  
I think 2019 when it was started,

**[00:28:34.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000343)  
hindi ko alam kung sino may problema dito.

**[00:28:36.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000344)  
NLRC ba? DBM ba? Meron ba

**[00:28:38.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000345)

**[00:28:41.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000346)  
...

DBM?

**[00:28:42.860] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000347)  
Ano ko, si Ms. Unyiga,

**[00:28:44.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000348)

**[00:28:45.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000349)  
Huwag ka magalala.

**[00:28:47.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000350)  
O baka DPWH din.

**[00:28:50.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000351)  
Isa rin yun sa mga tatalakayan.

**[00:28:52.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000352)  
And also,

**[00:28:54.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000353)  
recently, may mga

**[00:28:56.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000354)  
pumunta po sa tanggapang

**[00:28:58.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000355)  
sa Wanted sa Radyo, Mr. Chair,

**[00:29:00.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000356)  
even nag-email sa akin sa

**[00:29:01.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000357)  
office ko dito sa Senate,

**[00:29:04.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000358)  
itong PRC. As a matter of fact,

**[00:29:06.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000359)  
matagal na itong PRC na ito.

**[00:29:07.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000360)  
Saan ba yung mga taga-PRC dito?

**[00:29:09.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000361)  
Ma'am Zamora? Okay.

**[00:29:12.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000362)  
Matagal na may problema

**[00:29:13.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000363)  
kay Ma'am Zamora. If you remember,

**[00:29:15.460] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000364)

**[00:29:18.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000365)  
Palagi kong pinupunay yung PRC?

**[00:29:22.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000366)  
Basta, mamaya.

**[00:29:25.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000367)  
Saan dito yung assistant?

**[00:29:27.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000368)  
Assistant?

**[00:29:28.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000369)  
Yung commissioner mo.

**[00:29:30.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000370)  
Matanong ko lang muna.

**[00:29:32.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000371)  
Doktor ba siya?

**[00:29:34.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000372)  
I-made attorney ba siya?

**[00:29:36.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000373)  
Hindi siya. Seso ba siya?

**[00:29:38.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000374)  
Hindi siya seso?

**[00:29:40.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000375)  
So, hindi siya attorney, hindi siya seso.

**[00:29:43.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000376)  
Sorry, ah.

**[00:29:44.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000377)  
Dapat hindi siya sa pwestong yan.

**[00:29:46.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000378)  
And I'll tell you bakit dapat sibakin mo siya.

**[00:29:49.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000379)  
And I'll tell you why.

**[00:29:50.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000380)  
At sa ang daming kalukuhan nyo

**[00:29:52.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000381)  
sa PRC, ang daming yung kalukuhan

**[00:29:54.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000382)  
i-expose ko right here, right now.

**[00:29:56.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000383)  
Ito.

**[00:29:58.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000384)  
Nakikipagsabwatan

**[00:30:00.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000385)  
kayo sa mga

**[00:30:00.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000386)  
nagbibigay ng exams?

**[00:30:05.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000387)  
Kaya mga

**[00:30:06.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000388)  
regional director nyo, inaabot na ng dekada.

**[00:30:08.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000389)  
Ano ba yun? Hanggang kamatayan?

**[00:30:09.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000390)  
Hindi nyo nilishuffle?

**[00:30:11.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000391)  
Alam ko ba't hindi nilishuffle. Kasi

**[00:30:13.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000392)  
tumatanggap kayo sa mga

**[00:30:15.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000393)  
nagbibigay ng exam.

**[00:30:18.760] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000394)  
Meron kayong bagman.

**[00:30:20.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000395)  
Yung assistant commissioner nyo.

**[00:30:22.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000396)  
Si assistant commissioner,

**[00:30:24.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000397)  
for all I know, diba dapat

**[00:30:25.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000398)  
ano siya? Tawang yan? Yung

**[00:30:27.440] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000399)

**[00:30:30.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000400)  
I think

**[00:30:33.640] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-000401)  
it's permanent.

**[00:30:35.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000402)  
Is it permanent? Nakalagi sa batas.

**[00:30:37.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000403)  
But anyway, i-explain ko sa'yo mamaya kung bakit.

**[00:30:41.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000404)  
Anong tawag din sa

**[00:30:42.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000405)  
nagbibigay ng exam?

**[00:30:44.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000406)  
Review center, diba?

**[00:30:46.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000407)  
Okay.

**[00:30:48.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000408)  
Hindi sa mga board, diba? Review center.

**[00:30:50.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000409)  
Hindi, may mga review centers

**[00:30:52.060] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000410)  
kayo, pero mamaya

**[00:30:54.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000411)  
ako natatakayin. Mamaya ako sabihin

**[00:30:55.500] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000412)  
mga ebidensya ko.

**[00:30:57.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000413)  
Kasi ba't yung mga tao mo

**[00:31:00.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000414)  
mga regional directors mo, sa mga review centers

**[00:31:01.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000415)  
na hindi mo sinisibak-sibak o hindi nyo pinapalitan

**[00:31:04.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000416)  
for more than a decade na

**[00:31:05.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000417)  
nandun sa pwesto nila, kinakalawang,

**[00:31:07.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000418)  
nilalawa, nabupulok, na hindi pa rin

**[00:31:09.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000419)  
nilishapol. Why? Because, nalaman ko,

**[00:31:12.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000420)  
meron palang malaking katiwalan

**[00:31:13.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000421)  
dyan sa inyo. Example ko sa inyo,

**[00:31:15.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000422)  
yung doktor sa ICON, ilan ang napatay noon.

**[00:31:17.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000423)  
Hanggang ngayon, hindi nyo pa rin matatanggal-tanggalan

**[00:31:19.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000424)  
ng lisensya. Alam mo yung doktor?

**[00:31:22.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000425)  
Yung ICON?

**[00:31:24.040] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-000426)  
Yes po.

**[00:31:24.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000426-split-2)  
Tatlo na ata napatay noon.

**[00:31:26.180] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000427)  
Maraming nag-file mo nang para

**[00:31:27.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000428)  
matanggalan siya ng lisensya? Malpractice?

**[00:31:30.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000429)  
Ba't hindi nyo kayang tanggalan ng lisensya yun?

**[00:31:32.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000430)  
Because, may lagay sa inyo.

**[00:31:35.260] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000431)  
Exposed ko.

**[00:31:39.080] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000432)  
Kaya, mahirap

**[00:31:40.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000433)  
sa mga doktor na matatanggalan

**[00:31:41.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000434)  
ng lisensya because kasabwat

**[00:31:43.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000435)  
marami sa inyo dyan sa PRC?

**[00:31:46.160] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000436)  
Kaya pala, mas kinanoon pa,

**[00:31:47.980] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000437)  
even before I became a senator, Mr. President,

**[00:31:50.140] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000438)  
or Mr. Chairman,

**[00:31:51.820] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000439)  
nagreklamo sa akin, mga doktor, na

**[00:31:53.720] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000440)  
very glaring yung violation,

**[00:31:55.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000441)  
pangit yung servisyo, hindi.

**[00:31:57.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000442)  
Hindi maganda ang ginawang operation, kaya

**[00:31:59.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000443)  
namatay yung pasyente, na misdiagnosed

**[00:32:01.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000444)  
pasyente, nag-file kami

**[00:32:03.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000445)  
ng para medical

**[00:32:05.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000446)  
malpractice. Hindi!

**[00:32:08.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000447)  
Ito yung ICON,

**[00:32:09.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000448)  
yung clinic. Tatlo na ata

**[00:32:11.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000449)  
napatay na pasyente. Andami yung

**[00:32:13.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000450)  
nag-file ng para disbarment, para

**[00:32:15.280] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000451)  
matanggalan siya ng lisensya.

**[00:32:18.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000452)  
Wala pa, tatlo na napatay.

**[00:32:20.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000453)  
Gusto nyo sampung

**[00:32:21.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000454)  
mamatay muna? O baka gusto nyo isang daan mamatay

**[00:32:23.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000455)  
bago nyo tanggalan ng lisensya to?

**[00:32:25.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000456)  
Alam ko na bakit ayaw nyo tanggalan ng lisensya.

**[00:32:27.540] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000457)  
Kasi pasok kayo. Not you necessarily.

**[00:32:30.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000458)  
Ganun kayo sa PRC.

**[00:32:31.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000459)  
Mamaya, expose ko. Mamaya, Mr.

**[00:32:33.780] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000460)  
Chair.

**[00:32:38.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000461)  
Mamaya,

**[00:32:39.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000462)  
question and answer.

**[00:32:45.020] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000463)  
I'm sorry.

**[00:32:47.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000464)  
Secretary

**[00:32:47.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000465)  
Tolentino,

**[00:32:49.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000466)  
sorry,

**[00:32:50.800] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000467)

**[00:32:53.240] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000468)  
By the way, I'm very glad. Long time no see.

**[00:32:55.700] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000469)  
Thank you, Sec.

**[00:43:09.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000775)  
The next slide

**[00:43:11.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000776)  
provides a snapshot of the labor

**[00:43:13.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000777)  
and employment situation. The labor

**[00:43:15.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000778)  
force increased from 51.1 million

**[00:43:17.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000779)  
to 52.4 million,

**[00:43:19.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000780)  
but participation declined from

**[00:43:21.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000781)  
64.4 percent to 63.6

**[00:43:23.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000782)  
percent, indicating that labor

**[00:43:25.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000783)  
force growth is not keeping pace

**[00:43:27.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000784)  
with the working age population.

**[00:43:29.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000785)  
Unemployment rose sharply from

**[00:43:31.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000786)  
2.1 million to 3.1 million,

**[00:43:33.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000787)  
with the rate increasing from 4.2

**[00:43:35.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000788)  
percent to 6 percent, signaling

**[00:43:37.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000789)  
greater difficulty in absorbing new

**[00:43:39.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000790)  
job seekers. Employment increased

**[00:43:41.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000791)  
only marginally from 49 million

**[00:43:43.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000792)  
to 49.2 million, while

**[00:43:45.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000793)  
the employment rate fell from 95.8

**[00:43:47.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000794)  
percent to 94 percent,

**[00:43:49.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000795)  
suggesting that job creation has not

**[00:43:51.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000796)  
matched labor force growth.

**[00:43:53.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000797)  
Underemployment increased from

**[00:43:55.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000798)  
5.8 million to 6.3 million,

**[00:43:57.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000799)  
with the rate rising from 11.9

**[00:43:59.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000800)  
percent to 12.9 percent,

**[00:44:01.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000801)  
pointing to continuing concerns on

**[00:44:03.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000802)  
job quality, hours, and income

**[00:44:05.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000803)  
adequacy. Wage and salary

**[00:44:07.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000804)  
employment grew from 31.3

**[00:44:09.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000805)  
million to 32.5 million,

**[00:44:11.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000806)  
with its share increasing

**[00:44:13.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000807)  
from 63.2 percent

**[00:44:15.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000808)  
to 66.1 percent,

**[00:44:17.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000809)  
a positive shift towards more

**[00:44:19.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000810)  
structured employment. Youth

**[00:44:21.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000811)  
NEET, or not in employment, education,

**[00:44:23.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000812)  
and training increased from 0.9

**[00:44:25.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000813)  
million to 1.3 million,

**[00:44:27.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000814)  
with the rate rising from 12.8

**[00:44:29.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000815)  
percent to 15.2 percent,

**[00:44:31.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000816)  
highlighting the need to strengthen

**[00:44:33.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000817)

**[00:44:35.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000818)

**[00:44:37.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000819)  
Overall,

**[00:44:39.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000820)  
the labor market is expanding, but

**[00:44:41.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000821)

**[00:44:43.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000822)

**[00:44:47.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000824)

**[00:44:49.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000825)

**[00:49:44.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000971)  
Honorable Chair,

**[00:49:46.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000972)  
allow me to share the TUPAD flagship

**[00:49:47.840] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000973)  
innovations, our efforts to transform

**[00:49:50.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000974)  
TUPAD from temporary employment

**[00:49:51.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000975)  
to sustainable employment and livelihood.

**[00:49:55.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000976)  
The central direction

**[00:49:56.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000977)  
of this initiative is simple.

**[00:49:57.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000978)  
We want TUPAD to remain

**[00:49:59.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000979)  
an immediate safety net, but we also

**[00:50:01.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000980)  
want it to become a bridge towards

**[00:50:03.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000981)  
skills, enterprise, and more sustainable

**[00:50:05.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000982)  
employment. To strengthen

**[00:50:07.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000983)  
its impact beyond temporary work,

**[00:50:09.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000984)  
Department Order No. 239-E

**[00:50:12.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000985)  
Series of 2026, further

**[00:50:13.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000986)  
amending DO 239-23

**[00:50:16.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000987)  
enhances TUPAD

**[00:50:17.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000988)  
by creating integrated work-to-skills,

**[00:50:20.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000989)  
work-to-enterprise, and

**[00:50:21.780] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000990)  
work-to-employment pathways.

**[00:50:23.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000991)  
Under the 2026 TUPAD Program

**[00:50:25.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000992)  
Development Plan, temporary

**[00:50:27.660] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000993)  
employment becomes an entry point

**[00:50:29.600] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000994)  
rather than an end point.

**[00:50:31.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000995)  
A beneficiary who starts with short-term

**[00:50:33.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000996)  
community work may transition to skills

**[00:50:35.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000997)  
training and certification, workplace

**[00:50:37.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000998)  
exposure, regular or wage

**[00:50:39.460] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-000999)  
employment, livelihood and enterprise

**[00:50:41.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001000)  
opportunities, and other sustainable

**[00:50:43.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001001)  
economic pathways through stronger

**[00:50:45.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001002)  
convergence among DOLE programs

**[00:50:47.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001003)  
and partner institutions.

**[00:50:49.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001004)  
The enhanced framework therefore seeks to move

**[00:50:51.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001005)  
beneficiaries from temporary work

**[00:50:53.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001006)  
to lasting opportunities.

**[00:50:55.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001007)  
From Wallis Wallis and other short-term community

**[00:50:57.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001008)  
activities to skills,

**[00:50:59.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001009)  
certification, enterprise, and

**[00:51:01.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001010)  
employment. Beyond measuring days

**[00:51:03.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001011)  
worked and wages disbursed,

**[00:51:05.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001012)  
TUPAD will increasingly track verifiable

**[00:51:07.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001013)  
post-program outcomes

**[00:51:09.520] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001014)  
and economic transitions,

**[00:51:11.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001015)  
strengthening the program as a bridge from

**[00:51:13.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001016)  
immediate income support toward more

**[00:51:15.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001017)  
sustainable employment and livelihood.

**[00:51:17.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001018)  
For this presentation, we will highlight

**[00:51:19.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001019)  
four innovations, Skill Pass,

**[00:51:21.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001020)  
Sagip Trabaho, Tulong Aral,

**[00:51:23.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001021)  
and Trabahong Dagat, each addressing

**[00:51:25.420] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001022)  
a different transition opportunity for

**[00:51:27.360] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001023)  
TUPAD beneficiaries.

**[00:51:29.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001024)  
The diagram shows how we envision

**[00:51:31.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001025)  
the beneficiary journey under the

**[00:51:33.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001026)  
enhanced TUPAD framework.

**[00:51:35.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001027)  
We begin with immediate assistance because

**[00:51:37.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001028)  
providing temporary employment and income

**[00:51:39.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001029)  
support remains a fundamental function of

**[00:51:41.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001030)  
TUPAD. But instead of ending

**[00:51:43.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001031)  
the intervention after the payment of wages,

**[00:51:45.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001032)  
we proceed to assessment and profiling.

**[00:51:47.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001033)  
We identify the beneficiary's

**[00:51:49.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001034)  
skills, interests, needs,

**[00:51:51.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001035)  
and potential opportunities.

**[00:51:53.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001036)  
Based on that assessment, the beneficiary

**[00:51:55.460] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001037)  
may then proceed to an appropriate skill,

**[00:51:57.360] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001038)  
employment, livelihood, or education

**[00:51:59.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001039)  
intervention, followed by referral

**[00:52:01.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001040)  
or transition to partner programs

**[00:52:03.700] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001041)  
and services. The desired

**[00:52:05.520] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001042)  
endpoint is a verified next step,

**[00:52:07.920] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001043)  
whether employment, self-employment,

**[00:52:10.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001044)  
certification, further training,

**[00:52:11.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001045)  
education, or another sustainable

**[00:52:13.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001046)  
opportunity. And throughout

**[00:52:15.520] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001047)  
the entire pathway, monitoring,

**[00:52:17.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001048)  
evaluation, learning, and feedback

**[00:52:19.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001049)  
allow us to continuously improve

**[00:52:21.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001050)  
the program. The program

**[00:52:23.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001051)  
development plan recognizes that TUPAD

**[00:52:25.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001052)  
beneficiaries have different circumstances,

**[00:52:27.360] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001053)  
and therefore require different

**[00:52:29.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001054)  
pathways. Our target beneficiaries

**[00:52:31.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001055)  
include recent graduates,

**[00:52:33.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001056)  
first-time job seekers, and youth who need

**[00:52:35.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001057)  
work experience or employment facilitation,

**[00:52:38.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001058)  
economically disadvantaged,

**[00:52:39.840] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001059)  
displaced, underemployed,

**[00:52:41.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001060)  
and informal sector workers,

**[00:52:43.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001061)  
and workers and communities affected

**[00:52:45.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001062)  
by calamities, emergencies,

**[00:52:47.460] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001063)  
economic disruptions, or temporary

**[00:52:49.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001064)  
loss of employment or income,

**[00:52:51.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001065)  
even including returning

**[00:52:52.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001066)  
migrant workers and persons with

**[00:52:55.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001067)  
disabilities and workers in

**[00:52:57.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001068)  
vulnerable situations.

**[00:52:59.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001069)  
A key feature here is cross-referral,

**[00:53:02.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001070)  
where appropriate

**[00:53:03.920] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001071)  
qualified beneficiaries may

**[00:53:05.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001072)  
transition across the flagship innovations

**[00:53:07.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001073)  
and existing DOLE programs based

**[00:53:09.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001074)  
on their profile, eligibility,

**[00:53:11.600] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001075)  
competencies, needs, and available

**[00:53:13.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001076)  
opportunities. So rather than providing

**[00:53:15.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001077)  
a one-size-fits-all intervention,

**[00:53:17.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001078)  
we are creating a continuum of

**[00:53:19.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001079)  
assistance toward more sustainable

**[00:53:21.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001080)  
outcomes.

**[00:53:23.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001081)  
Our first innovation is SkillPass,

**[00:53:24.980] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001082)  
one credential, two programs.

**[00:53:27.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001083)  
SkillPass is designed to connect actual

**[00:53:29.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001084)  
work experience under TUPAD or GIP

**[00:53:31.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001085)  
with competency development and

**[00:53:33.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001086)  
TESDA certification. The beneficiary

**[00:53:35.500] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001087)  
receives a QR verifiable

**[00:53:37.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001088)  
credential documenting authorized

**[00:53:39.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001089)  
work experience and, where applicable,

**[00:53:41.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001090)  
TESDA training, assessment, and

**[00:53:43.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001091)  
certification. The objective is not

**[00:53:45.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001092)  
simply to issue another certificate.

**[00:53:47.520] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001093)  
The objective is to make the beneficiary's

**[00:53:49.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001094)  
experience visible, verifiable,

**[00:53:51.740] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001095)  
and useful for the next opportunity,

**[00:53:53.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001096)  
whether employment, enterprise,

**[00:53:55.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001097)  
TESDA certification, or

**[00:53:57.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001098)  
further training.

**[00:53:59.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001099)  
SkillPass begins with identification

**[00:54:01.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001100)  
and profiling. The DOLE determines

**[00:54:03.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001101)  
the appropriate work, skills, employment,

**[00:54:05.660] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001102)  
or enterprise pathway for the beneficiary.

**[00:54:08.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001103)  
Next is skills and work mapping,

**[00:54:09.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001104)  
where DOLE works with TESDA,

**[00:54:11.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001105)  
PESOS, LGUs, employers,

**[00:54:13.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001106)  
and other partners to determine whether

**[00:54:15.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001107)  
the TUPAD work assignment or

**[00:54:17.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001108)  
GIP internship can be linked to relevant

**[00:54:19.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001109)  
competencies. The beneficiary

**[00:54:21.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001110)  
then undertakes the approved TUPAD work

**[00:54:23.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001111)  
engagement or GIP internship under

**[00:54:25.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001112)  
existing program rules.

**[00:54:27.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001113)  
Where applicable, TESDA provides

**[00:54:29.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001114)  
or facilitates competency-based training,

**[00:54:31.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001115)  
assessment, and certification.

**[00:54:33.420] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001116)  
DOLE validates the beneficiary's

**[00:54:35.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001117)  
participation and work records, while

**[00:54:37.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001118)  
TESDA validates the corresponding training

**[00:54:39.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001119)  
and certification information.

**[00:54:41.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001120)  
These are consolidated into the SkillPass,

**[00:54:43.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001121)  
which, under the proposed pilot standard,

**[00:54:45.640] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001122)  
should be issued within 15 days from

**[00:54:47.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001123)  
completion or exit, subject to availability

**[00:54:49.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001124)  
of validated records.

**[00:54:51.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001125)  
But issuance is not the end. The final

**[00:54:53.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001126)  
step is referral and transition to

**[00:54:55.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001127)  
PESOS and employers for jobs,

**[00:54:57.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001128)  
to livelihood or enterprise assistance,

**[00:54:59.780] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001129)  
or to TESDA for further training

**[00:55:01.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001130)  
and higher-level certification.

**[00:55:03.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001131)  
This is the proposed SkillPass

**[00:55:04.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001132)  
Progress Report Card. It's intended to

**[00:55:07.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001133)  
consolidate the beneficiary's journey

**[00:55:08.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001134)  
from work engagement to training and

**[00:55:11.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001135)  
competency development to certification

**[00:55:13.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001136)  
and eventual transition. This gives

**[00:55:15.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001137)  
us a mechanism not only to document

**[00:55:17.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001138)  
what government provided, but also

**[00:55:19.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001139)  
to determine whether the beneficiary went

**[00:55:21.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001140)  
after the intervention. That is

**[00:55:23.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001141)  
important because the measure of success

**[00:55:25.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001142)  
should eventually move beyond the number

**[00:55:27.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001143)  
of beneficiaries served towards

**[00:55:29.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001144)  
the number of those successfully transitioned

**[00:55:31.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001145)  
to a better economic opportunity.

**[00:55:33.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001146)  
Our second innovation is

**[00:55:35.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001147)  
Sagip Trabaho, anchored on a

**[00:55:37.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001148)  
very straightforward principle,

**[00:55:39.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001149)  
keep the worker working. Instead

**[00:55:41.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001150)  
of waiting for a worker to lose his

**[00:55:43.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001151)  
or her job before government intervenes,

**[00:55:45.740] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001152)  
Sagip Trabaho is designed as

**[00:55:47.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001153)  
a time-bound job preservation and

**[00:55:49.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001154)  
employment continuity intervention

**[00:55:51.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001155)  
for qualified distressed MSMEs

**[00:55:53.780] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001156)  
facing temporary shocks

**[00:55:55.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001157)  
that could result in worker displacement,

**[00:55:57.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001158)  
or business closure. Qualified

**[00:55:59.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001159)  
MSMEs must apply and submit a

**[00:56:01.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001160)  
job preservation and business continuity

**[00:56:03.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001161)  
plan. Temporary wage

**[00:56:05.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001162)  
support may cover a maximum of

**[00:56:07.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001163)  
35 qualified workers per

**[00:56:09.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001164)  
participating employer, complemented

**[00:56:11.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001165)  
where applicable by ECC

**[00:56:13.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001166)  
or ECP assistance, skills

**[00:56:15.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001167)  
upgrading, productivity measures,

**[00:56:17.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001168)  
rehabilitation, return to work, and

**[00:56:19.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001169)  
business continuity support.

**[00:56:21.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001170)  
The Sagip Trabaho business continuity

**[00:56:23.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001171)  
plan is a simplified action plan

**[00:56:24.980] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001172)  
showing how participating MSMEs

**[00:56:27.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001173)  
will use temporary government assistance

**[00:56:29.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001174)  
to preserve jobs, address

**[00:56:31.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001175)  
operational challenges, and support

**[00:56:32.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001176)  
business recovery. The plan

**[00:56:35.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001177)  
ensures that Sagip Trabaho assistance

**[00:56:37.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001178)  
is tied to concrete job preservation

**[00:56:39.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001179)  
and business continuity measures

**[00:56:41.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001180)  
such as productive work,

**[00:56:42.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001181)  
maintenance, retraining, skills upgrading,

**[00:56:45.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001182)  
and return to work activities.

**[00:56:47.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001183)  
For participating workers,

**[00:56:49.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001184)  
assistance may include

**[00:56:50.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001185)  
temporary wage support for up to 90 days,

**[00:56:53.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001186)  
occupational safety and health

**[00:56:55.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001187)  
protection, employees' compensation,

**[00:56:57.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001188)  
program benefits, and services

**[00:56:58.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001189)  
where applicable, workplace

**[00:57:00.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001190)  
retraining and skills development,

**[00:57:03.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001191)  
employment facilitation, and referral to

**[00:57:04.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001192)  
other interventions. But government

**[00:57:07.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001193)  
assistance does not remove the employer's

**[00:57:09.240] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001194)  
statutory responsibilities.

**[00:57:11.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001195)  
Participating employers remain

**[00:57:13.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001196)  
responsible for applicable SSS,

**[00:57:15.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001197)  
PhilHealth, and Pag-IBIG contributions,

**[00:57:17.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001198)  
as well as other obligations required

**[00:57:19.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001199)  
by law. So the intervention

**[00:57:20.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001200)  
is designed as a temporary recovery

**[00:57:22.880] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001201)  
window, not a replacement

**[00:57:24.840] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001202)  
for the employer's regular obligations.

**[00:57:27.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001203)  
The process begins with the

**[00:57:28.980] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001204)  
MSME itself applying to the concerned

**[00:57:31.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001205)  
DOLE Regional Office. The application

**[00:57:33.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001206)  
must include job preservation and

**[00:57:34.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001207)  
business continuity plan, identifying

**[00:57:36.840] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001208)  
the disruption, affected workers,

**[00:57:38.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001209)  
proposed activities, recovery measures,

**[00:57:41.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001210)  
and worker retention commitments.

**[00:57:43.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001211)  
The DOLE then validates the actual

**[00:57:44.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001212)  
condition of the enterprise, the eligibility

**[00:57:46.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001213)  
of the workers, employer compliance,

**[00:57:49.300] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001214)  
counterpart commitments, and very

**[00:57:51.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001215)  
importantly, whether there is a reasonable

**[00:57:53.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001216)  
prospect for operational recovery.

**[00:57:55.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001217)  
Only after this assessment will

**[00:57:56.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001218)  
DOLE determine whether temporary assistance

**[00:57:58.720] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001219)  
is reasonably expected to prevent

**[00:58:00.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001220)  
displacement or closure.

**[00:58:03.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001221)  
Once approved, there will be a tripartite

**[00:58:04.920] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001222)  
undertaking among DOLE, the employer,

**[00:58:07.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001223)  
and participating workers.

**[00:58:08.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001224)  
This will clearly specify the workers

**[00:58:10.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001225)  
covered, assistance period,

**[00:58:12.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001226)  
productive activities, wage support,

**[00:58:14.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001227)  
employer counterpart, supervision

**[00:58:16.480] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001228)  
arrangements, monitoring requirements,

**[00:58:18.720] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001229)  
and post-assistance retention commitment.

**[00:58:21.200] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001230)  
During implementation,

**[00:58:22.600] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001231)  
workers remain productively engaged

**[00:58:24.360] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001232)  
through approved workplace tasks, maintenance,

**[00:58:26.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001233)  
retraining, or other authorized activities.

**[00:58:29.640] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001234)  
TUPAD funds may support

**[00:58:31.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001235)  
eligible beneficiary wage payments

**[00:58:33.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001236)  
for an approved period

**[00:58:34.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001237)  
of up to 90 days.

**[00:58:36.920] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001238)  
Monitoring is critical because

**[00:58:38.360] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001239)  
sagip trabaho is not simply a wage support

**[00:58:40.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001240)  
mechanism. DOLE will monitor

**[00:58:42.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001241)  
attendance, actual work performed,

**[00:58:44.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001242)  
employer counterpart, labor standards

**[00:58:46.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001243)  
compliance, implementation of the business

**[00:58:48.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001244)  
continuity plan, and progress toward

**[00:58:50.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001245)  
recovery. Ultimately, what we

**[00:58:52.720] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001246)  
want to know is, did the intervention

**[00:58:54.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001247)  
actually preserve the job?

**[00:58:56.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001248)  
Did the enterprise recover?

**[00:58:58.500] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001249)  
And was the worker able to remain employed

**[00:59:00.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001250)  
after government assistance ended?

**[00:59:02.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001251)  
That is the outcome against which

**[00:59:04.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001252)  
sagip trabaho should be assessed.

**[00:59:06.640] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001253)  
Our third innovation is tulong aral,

**[00:59:09.420] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001254)  
which links temporary employment

**[00:59:10.980] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001255)  
with an important social need,

**[00:59:12.800] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001256)  
foundational learning. Qualified

**[00:59:14.760] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001257)  
education graduates, licensure examination

**[00:59:17.080] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001258)  
passers, and academic mentors

**[00:59:18.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001259)  
may be engaged to provide small group

**[00:59:20.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001260)  
remedial support in reading, writing,

**[00:59:23.180] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001261)  
and mathematics for grades 1

**[00:59:24.820] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001262)  
to 3 learners. Before the

**[00:59:26.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001263)  
deployment, mentors undergo a 10-day

**[00:59:28.700] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001264)  
capacity-building program covering

**[00:59:30.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001265)  
literacy, writing, and language skills,

**[00:59:33.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001266)  
mathematics and numeracy,

**[00:59:34.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001267)  
age-appropriate money skills,

**[00:59:36.660] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001268)  
learning facilitation, child protection,

**[00:59:39.100] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001269)  
and documentation.

**[00:59:40.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001270)  
DepEd oversees the learning content and

**[00:59:42.720] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001271)  
learner selection, and sessions are subject

**[00:59:44.860] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001272)  
to child protection safeguards and a

**[00:59:46.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001273)  
maximum of 1 is to 5 mentor-to-learner

**[00:59:49.060] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001274)  
ratio. Implementation

**[00:59:51.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001275)  
starts with DepEd identifying

**[00:59:52.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001276)  
participating public schools and grades

**[00:59:54.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001277)  
1 to 3 learners requiring supplementary

**[00:59:56.680] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001278)  
learning support. DepEd and

**[00:59:58.640] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001279)  
participating schools then...

**[01:00:00.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001280)  
endorse qualified education graduates, teacher licensure passers, and other academic mentors.

**[01:00:05.740] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001281)  
The DOLE subsequently profiles and validates these endorsed mentors

**[01:00:09.280] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001282)  
based on existing TUPAD eligibility requirements.

**[01:00:12.740] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001283)  
This division of responsibility is important.

**[01:00:15.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001284)  
DepEd determines the educational need while the DOLE determines TUPAD eligibility.

**[01:00:20.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001285)  
Before mentors enter the classroom or learning venue,

**[01:00:22.840] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001286)  
they undergo suitability and child protection screening and execute a child protection commitment.

**[01:00:28.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001287)  
Qualified mentors then complete the 10-day short course or capacity building program.

**[01:00:33.220] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001288)  
Only after completing these requirements are they assigned to participating schools

**[01:00:37.020] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001289)  
or DepEd-approved venues with proper supervision, schedules, learner assignments,

**[01:00:42.520] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001290)  
and maximum one mentor for every five learners.

**[01:00:45.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001291)  
Before the learning intervention begins, parent or guardian consent must be secured

**[01:00:49.500] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001292)  
and DepEd or school conducts or validates the learner's baseline assessment.

**[01:00:54.540] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001293)  
Mentors then conduct structured and supervised small group learning sessions

**[01:00:58.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001294)  
using DepEd-approved materials.

**[01:01:00.640] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001295)  
The DOLE and DepEd jointly monitor implementation,

**[01:01:03.440] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001296)  
including attendance, sessions conducted, learner participation, and accomplishments.

**[01:01:08.040] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001297)  
This ensures that tulong-aral is not simply an employment activity for the mentor.

**[01:01:12.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001298)  
It must also produce a properly supervised learning support intervention for the child.

**[01:01:16.900] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001299)  
At the end of the intervention, DepEd or the school conducts or validates an exit assessment

**[01:01:21.960] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001300)  
to determine the learner's progress in foundational learning.

**[01:01:25.720] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001301)  
Qualified mentors receive a tulong-aral certification,

**[01:01:28.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001302)  
certificate, and the program also provides recognition for participation and completion.

**[01:01:33.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001303)  
For the mentors, however, we again do not want the intervention to end there.

**[01:01:36.880] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001304)  
The DOLE, working with PESOS, DepEd, and other partners,

**[01:01:41.140] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001305)  
will facilitate ladderized referral to further training, employment, internship, livelihood, or other opportunities.

**[01:01:47.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001306)  
Our fourth innovation is Trabahong Dagat,

**[01:01:49.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001307)  
designed specifically for coastal communities, fisherfolk, households, and coastal youth.

**[01:01:54.880] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001308)  
During closed fishing seasons and other seasonal conditions,

**[01:01:58.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001309)  
climate-related livelihood disruptions,

**[01:02:00.380] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001310)  
eligible beneficiaries may receive temporary Tupad-supported work.

**[01:02:04.120] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001311)  
But the intervention can go further through skills training, certification,

**[01:02:07.740] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001312)  
starter tools and equipment, enterprise assistance, market linkage, and employment facilitation.

**[01:02:12.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001313)  
The proposed initial pilot areas are Zamboanga Peninsula and Zambales.

**[01:02:17.580] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001314)  
The intention is to use periods of livelihood disruption not only to provide temporary income,

**[01:02:22.500] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001315)  
but also to build alternative and complementary economic opportunities for coastal households.

**[01:02:28.000] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001316)  
Trabahong Dagat has three specialized tracks.

**[01:02:31.340] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001317)  
Dagat Skills and Services for Track 1 focuses on competencies such as

**[01:02:35.920] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001318)  
seamanship, maritime safety, boat and engine maintenance, net repair, and shore-based maritime support.

**[01:02:42.500] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001319)  
ISDA Skills and Production for Track 2 covers aquaculture, sustainable practices,

**[01:02:47.940] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001320)  
fishing processing, food safety, hatchery operations, and post-harvest activities.

**[01:02:52.460] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001321)  
Track 3, or Blue Enterprise Development, supports qualified beneficiaries

**[01:02:57.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001322)  
who are ready to start their business.

**[01:02:57.980] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001323)  
To organize into fisheries-related micro-enterprises with starter support and operational coaching.

**[01:03:04.600] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001324)  
Beneficiaries are not placed into one uniform intervention.

**[01:03:08.160] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001325)  
The pathway depends on local community opportunities, skills, and economic potential.

**[01:03:13.620] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001326)  
Implementation begins with area and fisheries profiling.

**[01:03:16.880] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001327)  
DOLE coordinates with DA, BFAR, TESDA, and LGUs to understand local fisheries,

**[01:03:22.400] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001328)  
seasonal work patterns, skills needs, facilities, and available employment.

**[01:03:27.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001330)  
Yes, Senator Raffy.

**[01:03:28.940] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001331)  
Kasi meron pa tayong 3 o'clock sa plenary.

**[01:03:30.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001332)  
I don't know how long.

**[01:03:31.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001333)  
Kung isang oras pa siya, di plenary muna tayo.

**[01:03:35.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001334)  
Buti na lang, wala si...

**[01:03:37.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001335)  
Ay, mga ilan ito?

**[01:03:39.420] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001336)  
15 slides pa po.

**[01:03:40.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001337)  
How many slides?

**[01:03:41.660] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001338)  
15 slides pa po.

**[01:03:42.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001339)  
15?

**[01:03:43.260] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001340)  
Yes, po.

**[01:03:44.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001341)  
How long is your presentation?

**[01:03:46.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001342)  
Do you think you can do a one-hour presentation?

**[01:03:49.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001343)  
I don't think you can...

**[01:03:51.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001344)  
Kasi, Mr. Chair, alis ako in 30 minutes.

**[01:03:53.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001345)  
Kung abot 30 minutes, di...

**[01:03:55.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001346)  
Anyway, we have your presentation.

**[01:03:57.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001347)  
Can you just give us the...

**[01:03:58.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001348)  
Because we have heard about the five...

**[01:04:01.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001349)  
I mean, the skills pass,

**[01:04:03.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001350)  
Sagip Trabaho, Tulong Aral,

**[01:04:04.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001351)  
and this Trabahong Dagat.

**[01:04:07.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001352)

**[01:04:09.560] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001353)  
Yes, po.

Trabahong dagat.

**[01:04:11.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001355)  
And then, what's next?

**[01:04:12.700] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001356)

**[01:04:14.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001357)  
Yeah.

**[01:04:14.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001358)  
Can you just give us the one...

**[01:04:18.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001359)  
The most important part, probably,

**[01:04:22.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001360)  
the budget proposal,

**[01:04:24.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001361)  
which has now decreased.

**[01:04:27.320] DOLE Planning Service Director Patrick P. Patriwirawan Jr.** (asr-001362)  
Yeah.

**[01:04:28.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001363)  
Ayan.

**[01:04:29.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001364)  
Tsaka yung isa pa, ano yung next?

**[01:04:32.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001365)  
Just to give us a bird's eye view

**[01:04:34.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001366)  
of your budget,

**[01:04:36.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001367)  
which is

**[01:04:37.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001368)  
decreased by

**[01:04:41.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001369)  
14.08 billion.

**[01:04:46.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001370)  
Is that correct?

**[01:04:48.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001371)  
From 61.81 to 47.73 billion.

**[01:04:54.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001372)  
That's correct?

**[01:04:55.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001373)  
Yeah.

**[01:04:55.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001374)  
Anyway,

**[01:04:56.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001375)  
just submit to the committee

**[01:04:58.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001376)  
so we can proceed with the questioning.

**[01:05:03.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001377)  
Thank you, Director Patrick.

**[01:05:04.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001378)  
I'll give the floor to Senator Rafi Tulfo.

**[01:05:07.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001379)  
Your Honor, you have the floor.

**[01:05:09.300] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001380)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:05:10.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001381)  
Umpisan ko muna kay Secretary Tolentino.

**[01:05:14.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001382)  
Secretary Tolentino,

**[01:05:17.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001383)  
maraming mga nagreklamo sa akin,

**[01:05:19.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001384)  
kanina nabanggit ko nga ito,

**[01:05:20.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001385)  
mga regional director ng PRC.

**[01:05:23.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001386)  
Bakit hindi mo nilishuffle

**[01:05:24.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001387)  
yung iba sa kanila?

**[01:05:26.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001388)  
Mag-retire na lang,

**[01:05:27.640] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001389)  
nandoon pa rin,

**[01:05:28.400] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001390)  
dekadekada na.

**[01:05:29.480] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001391)  
May kasabihin yung

**[01:05:30.220] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001392)  
familiarity breeds contempt.

**[01:05:31.680] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001393)  
Meaning, wala nang respetuhan,

**[01:05:33.200] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001394)  
sila-sila magsasabwata na.

**[01:05:34.900] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001395)  
Wala na.

**[01:05:35.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001396)  
Gulo-gulo na.

**[01:05:36.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001397)  
Ba't hindi nililishuffle?

**[01:05:38.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001398)  
Nung pag-upo ng pag-upo mo,

**[01:05:39.620] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001399)  
ba't hindi ka nagbago ng polisya?

**[01:05:42.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001400)  
Tanggalin yung mga dati na?

**[01:05:43.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001401)  
Your Honor,

**[01:05:44.840] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001402)  
sangayon po sa umiiral na batas,

**[01:05:47.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001403)  
ang relationship po ng DOLE

**[01:05:50.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001404)  
at ng PRC ay

**[01:05:53.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001405)  
policymaking lang po

**[01:05:54.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001406)  
yung alignment noon.

**[01:05:56.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001407)  
At wala po sa

**[01:05:57.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001408)  
horesdiksyon ng kalihim

**[01:05:59.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001409)  
ng kagawaran na mag-reshuffle.

**[01:06:02.840] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001410)  
Ito po ay...

**[01:06:04.100] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001411)  
So, it is beyond your

**[01:06:05.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001412)  
powers na para

**[01:06:07.120] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001413)  
i-reshuffle ito mga

**[01:06:08.580] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001414)  
direkto sa PRC na dekadekada

**[01:06:11.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001415)  
na nasa pwesto?

**[01:06:13.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001416)  
Opo, Your Honor,

**[01:06:15.960] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001417)  
yung PRC mismo

**[01:06:17.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001418)  
ang nag-reshuffle.

**[01:06:20.060] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-001419)  
Sorry, John.

**[01:06:20.740] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001420)  
Okay, PRC.

**[01:06:23.660] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001421)  
Ba't ganon? Marami sa inyo mga regional

**[01:06:25.380] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001422)  
directors.

**[01:06:26.320] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001423)  
Na dekada na

**[01:06:28.340] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001424)  
sa pwesto at hindi pa rin

**[01:06:29.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001425)  
na-reshuffle. Ano ang reason behind?

**[01:06:32.960] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001426)  
Kasi para sa akin

**[01:06:34.000] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001427)  
at sa ibang mga government positions,

**[01:06:37.040] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001428)  
maski sa PNP,

**[01:06:38.560] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001429)  
two years, three years lang yung

**[01:06:39.920] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001430)  
kanilang mag-stay sa isang position,

**[01:06:42.420] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001431)  
yung tour of duty kung tawagin.

**[01:06:44.520] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001432)  
Pero dito sa inyo, parang

**[01:06:45.880] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001433)  
lifetime hanggang sa mag-retire?

**[01:06:48.360] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-001434)  
Why is that?

**[01:06:49.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001435)  
Senator Tulfo,

**[01:06:51.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001436)  
may we just ask the chairperson

**[01:06:54.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001437)  
if that is really the case?

**[01:06:56.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001438)  
Your regional directors

**[01:06:57.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001439)  
had been there for decades?

**[01:07:00.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-001440)  
Is that correct?

**[01:07:02.260] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-001441)  
May I, with the permission

**[01:07:04.400] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-001442)  
of the good secretary,

**[01:07:05.920] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-001443)  
Mr. Chair, if I may clarify,

**[01:07:08.660] PRC Chairperson Charito A. Zamora** (asr-001444)  
if I may explain,

Yes.
