**[01:39:50.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002686)  
Allow me to deliver a manifestation regarding the proposed budgets of the DOLE, Department of Labor and Employment and its attached agencies and corporations.

**[01:40:01.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002687)  
Gusto ko lang lang pong batiin lahat.

**[01:40:04.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002688)  
Institute for Labor Studies, ILS, National Conciliation and Mediation Board, NCMB, National Labor Relations Commission, NLRC. Ma'am.

**[01:40:15.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002689)  
National Wages and Productivity Commission, NWPC, and PRC, Professional Regulation Commission. Ma'am.

**[01:40:28.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002690)  
Occupational Safety and Health Center, Employees Compensation Commission.

**[01:40:33.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002691)  
Sa lahat po ng attached agencies, magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[01:40:36.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002692)  
At salamat po sa inyong servisyo.

**[01:40:38.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002693)  
Mr. Chair, for 2027, the proposed expenditure program of DOLE is 47.

**[01:40:43.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002694)  
47.726 billion equivalent to only 0.66 of the 7.2 trillion total national expenditure program.

**[01:40:59.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002695)  
Sobrang liit po ng share ng DOLE sa kabuhang national budget ngayong taong ito.

**[01:41:04.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002696)  
Kung baga sa cake, icing lang po ito. Napaka-liit.

**[01:41:10.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002697)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:41:13.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002698)  
May steadfast advocate for the welfare of every Filipino worker.

**[01:41:17.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002699)  
I support the department in fulfilling its mandate to protect the rights, promote the welfare, and improve the working conditions of our labor force.

**[01:41:28.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002700)  
May katanungan lang po ako, Mr. Chair, sa ating sekretary.

**[01:41:32.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002701)  
I would like to commend the department for its recent wage order under which minimum wage earners in the National Capital Region

**[01:41:41.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002702)  
will receive a 60 peso daily wage increase.

**[01:41:46.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002703)  
Ito po yung naging controversial nung mga nakarang buwan, nakarang araw.

**[01:41:52.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002704)  
Dagdag tulong po ito sa mahigit 1.1 million minimum wage earners in Metro Manila.

**[01:41:58.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002705)  
I hope that the department will continue to monitor compliance with the wage order and provide guidance to both workers and employers on its proper implementation.

**[01:42:08.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002706)  
We also hope that the circumstances that previously resulted in a delayed

**[01:42:13.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002707)  
the implementation of the wage increase will not happen again so that the benefits intended for our workers will not be further delayed.

**[01:42:21.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002708)  
At bawat piso, bawat sentimo po, lalong-lalo na po sa mga ordinaryong manggagawa ay napakahalaga po sa kanila.

**[01:42:29.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002709)  
Lalong-lalo na po sa mga isang kahig, isang tuka nating kababayan.

**[01:42:32.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002710)  
Yun po ang unahin natin na bigyan ng konsiderasyon at priority.

**[01:42:36.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002711)  
At sana rin po, hindi lamang Metro Manila ang ating pagtuunan ng pansin, tingnan rin po natin ang kalagayan ng mga manggagawa sa iba't ibang regyon,

**[01:42:44.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002712)  
lalo na sa mga lugar kung saan mas marami ang nangangailangan ng dagdag na suporta.

**[01:42:49.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002713)  
We hope that the department can take a more encompassing approach in addressing wage concerns

**[01:42:57.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002714)  
so that more Filipino workers wherever they may be in the country can benefit from meaningful wage increases and better economic opportunities.

**[01:43:05.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002715)  
Alam niyo po, Mr. Chair, matagal na po ang panawagan ng mga Pilipino ang pagtaas ng minimum wage sa bansa.

**[01:43:10.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002716)  
Patuloy ang pagtaas ng presyo ng mga bilihin dahil mas mabilis pa ang pagtataas ng bilihin kaysa sa pagtataas ng sahod.

**[01:43:22.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002717)  
Masasabi natin na mismatch pa rin ang pagitan ng sahod at presyo ng bilihin at mababa pa rin ang purchasing power ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:43:30.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002718)  
Kaya naman, inihihain rin po natin dito sa Senado, itong Senate Bill number 8.

**[01:43:35.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002719)  
175 na naglalayong magbibigay ng dagdag 100 peso sa daily minimum wage across the board para matulungan ang mga magagawa na makasabay sa patuloy na pagtaas ng presyo ng pangunahing bilihin at masiguro ang disenteng pamumuhay para sa kanilang pamilya.

**[01:43:55.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002720)  
At parate ko nga sinasabi sa mga malalaking negosyante, kung malaki naman po ang inyong kita, kung di naman po masakit sa inyo, sana po ipamahagin nyo na lang po ito, lalong-lalo na po sa mga maliliit na magagawa na isang kahig, isang tuka lang po ang kanilang kinikita.

**[01:44:12.480] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002721)  
Mr. Chair, may I ask the good secretary, the Regional Tripartite Wages and Productivity Board for the National Capital Region,

**[01:44:21.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002722)  
granted an 85 daily minimum wage increase to be implemented.

**[01:44:25.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002723)  
Ito yung naka-TRO. At kung tutusin, 85 na nga lang po ito, mas mababa pa kumpara sa isinasulong nating 100 peso wage increase. Ano na bang nangyari dito? At ano ang update nito, Mr. Secretary?

**[01:44:40.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002724)  
Yes, Secretary T. Lentino.

**[01:44:42.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002725)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chair, Senator Bongo.

**[01:44:45.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002726)  
Pagaya po nung nabanggit nyo, kasama nyo po ako sa pagsusulong ng mas mataas na sahod para sa ating manggagawa.

**[01:44:54.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002727)  
Pumerma din po ako.

**[01:44:55.120] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002728)  
Nandito pa ako doon sa 100 peso increase noong 19 Congress.

**[01:45:00.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002729)  
Tungkol po sa status ng Wage Order No. 27, meron po itong kinakaharap na limang kaso.

**[01:45:07.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002730)  
Yung isang kaso po ay sa Branch 152 ng PASIG kung saan merong na-issue yung status quo order,

**[01:45:17.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002731)  
tapos yung pasunod, TRO, tapos injunction.

**[01:45:21.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002732)  
Pangalawang kaso po ay nasa Nabotas RTC at ang dalawang kaso po na meron po dalawang kaso nakasampang ngayon

**[01:45:31.100] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002733)  
pending sa Court of Appeals.

**[01:45:33.480] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002734)  
Yung isa po ang nag-file ay isang employer at yung isa kung hindi ako nagkakamali ay employees group naman.

**[01:45:41.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002735)  
Meron din po isang nakabimbi na kaso sa Supreme Court.

**[01:45:44.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002736)  
Lahat po ito ay hindi po namin alam kung kailan matatapos isa-isa.

**[01:45:51.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002737)  
Kaya ano pa? Dalawa po ang hindi namin alam.

**[01:45:55.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002738)  
Hindi namin alam kung kailan matatapos.

**[01:45:57.980] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002739)  
Pangalawa, hindi namin alam kung ano magiging risulta.

**[01:46:01.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002740)  
Kung ito ba ay magiging pabor o hindi pabor o magtatagal hanggang Disyembre o 2027.

**[01:46:10.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002741)  
Kaya nga po, Your Honor, minarapat namin, bagamat hindi pa bahagi ng inyong tanong,

**[01:46:17.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002742)  
na minarapat ng Wage Board na isulong.

**[01:46:21.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002743)  
At dumaan sa prosesong tama sa ngayon sa batas, yung Wage Order 28.

**[01:46:27.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002744)  
Yung Wage Order 28 ay 60 pesos na implemented noong September 26, 2026, noong nakaraan linggo.

**[01:46:37.880] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002745)  
Ang tanong po ng marami at siguro baka bumabagabag din sa kaisipan ng iba,

**[01:46:43.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002746)  
e ba ano pa, paano mangyayari doon sa 85 at doon sa bagong 60?

**[01:46:48.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002747)  
Ang dapat po siguro nating isipin,

**[01:46:51.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002748)  
ay yung Wage Order 28 ay pumatong at nagsimula doon sa Wage Order 26

**[01:47:00.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002749)  
na umiiral bago i-implement si 28.

**[01:47:06.580] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002750)  
Yun po yung umiiral before September 26.

**[01:47:09.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002751)  
So, yung mga nagtatanong bakit bumaba, wala pong binaba.

**[01:47:13.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002752)  
Kasi yung umpong 26 ang umiiral.

**[01:47:16.840] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002753)  
So, from 26 na 695 pesos,

**[01:47:20.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002754)

**[01:47:25.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002755)  
Dahil si 27 ay nakakahon po sa husgado na hindi natin alam kung kailan matatapos

**[01:47:33.160] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002756)  
at ano po yung magiging risulta.

**[01:47:36.700] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002757)  
Kaya may narapat po sa ngayon din sa batas na ipasa yung Wage Order 27 ng Wage Board

**[01:47:43.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002758)  
at ito po ay kinatigan in a firm ng National Wages and Productivity Commission.

**[01:47:48.460] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002759)  
At simula nga noong September,

**[01:47:50.220] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002760)  
26 ay umiiral na.

**[01:47:52.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002761)  
So, ito pong ngayong araw pong ngayon ay at renta,

**[01:47:56.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002762)  
dapat po yung 26, 27, 26, 28, 29, 30,

**[01:48:03.900] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002763)  
halos apat na araw ay kasama na po dapat sa pay slip ng ating mga minimum wage earners.

**[01:48:10.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002764)  
At pagdating po ng 15 ay kasama na rin talaga dapat.

**[01:48:14.000] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002765)  
So, 60 pesos po.

**[01:48:15.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002766)  
60 pesos po.

**[01:48:16.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002767)  
Dito sa Metro Manila.

**[01:48:18.300] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002768)  
Metro Manila.

**[01:48:18.800] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002769)  
National Capital Region.

**[01:48:20.220] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002770)  
Ang tataasan ng kanilang daily minimum.

**[01:48:26.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002771)  
Yes, yes po.

**[01:48:27.540] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002772)  
At yun po ay pagtinuos natin sa loob ng isang buwan at ito ay 26 days,

**[01:48:32.600] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002773)

**[01:48:36.900] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002774)  
At yung tanong nyo rin kanina para na-daplisan nyo ay patungkol sa ibang regyon,

**[01:48:42.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002775)  
effective October 14 sa Central Visayas,

**[01:48:46.320] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002776)  
si Bubuhol at sinama pa po yung dalawang bahagi.

**[01:48:49.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002777)  
Ngayon ang Negros Island Region, dahil hindi pa po buo yung wage board sa Negros Island Region,

**[01:48:56.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002778)

**[01:48:59.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002779)  
ay magkakaroon sila ng karagdagang 42 pesos, effective October 14.

**[01:49:04.500] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002780)  
At sa loob ng isang buwan, yung mga kababayan natin sa Central Visayas ay makakatanggap ng 1,092 pesos monthly increase.

**[01:49:13.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002781)  
Ito po ay aabutin ng 385,000 minimum wage.

**[01:49:19.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002782)

**[01:49:31.120] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002783)

**[01:49:40.140] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002784)  
So pakiulit lang po, aside sa NCR, ano ang regyon po yun?

**[01:49:45.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002785)  
Yung Region 7 at bahagi po ng NIR.

**[01:49:49.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002786)  
NIR, ay sa Negros.

**[01:49:53.240] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002787)  
Ito pong napipinto na, malapit-lapit na, nag-uusap na,

**[01:49:59.080] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002788)

**[01:50:05.280] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002789)  
Hindi ko lang po mabanggit pa kung ano yung halaga dahil hindi pa naman napapublished

**[01:50:10.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002790)

**[01:50:15.740] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002791)

**[01:50:19.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002792)  
Yung Dabao po, nauna po yun, yung second tranche.

**[01:50:23.560] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002793)  
Nagkaroon na rin po sa Dabao nitong mga nakaraang buwan.

**[01:50:28.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002795)

**[01:50:32.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002796)

**[01:50:35.340] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002797)

**[01:50:39.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002798)

**[01:50:42.800] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002799)

**[01:50:45.620] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002800)

**[01:50:49.780] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002801)

**[01:50:55.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002802)  
Dun palang gumagalaw po yun, Your Honor.

**[01:50:57.200] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002803)  
Maraming salamat. Regardless of ano yung amount, mas alam nyo po yun.

**[01:51:02.040] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002804)  
Dahil mas alam nyo yung presyo ng bilihin dun, yung cost of living sa areas

**[01:51:07.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002805)

**[01:51:10.660] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002806)  
Pero sabi ko nga, bawat piso, bawat sentimo na pagdagdag sahod

**[01:51:15.820] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002807)

**[01:51:19.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002808)  
Sana po patuloy nyo itong tututukan.

**[01:51:24.020] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002809)  
At sabi ko nga kanina, sa mga malalaking negosyante,

**[01:51:28.360] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002810)  
kung hindi naman po, hindi nyo naman po ikakalugi,

**[01:51:31.420] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002811)  
hindi naman po malaking kawalan sa inyo,

**[01:51:33.780] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002812)

**[01:51:37.580] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002813)  
na makatulong po sa mga may hirap nating manggagawa.

**[01:51:44.100] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002814)  
Salamat po. Sana patuloy nyo itong tututukan.

**[01:51:48.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-002815)  
Another topic lang po.

**[01:51:49.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002816)  
Can we go to another topic, Senator Bongo?

**[01:51:52.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002817)  
I think this is very crucial and a lot of people are waiting to discuss this particular issue.

**[01:51:59.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002818)  
And if I may butt in, first, we'd like to put on record that we indeed passed a resolution

**[01:52:07.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002819)

**[01:52:17.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002820)  
And then, of course...

**[01:52:19.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002821)  
Of course, we'd like to put on record that then Senator Francis Tolentino,

**[01:52:24.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002822)  
Senator Bongo and this representation worked together in passing the measure

**[01:52:31.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002823)  
that would give additional 100 peso increase for our workers.

**[01:52:35.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002824)  
We were able to pass it on third and final reading here in this institution in the Senate.

**[01:52:41.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002825)  
But unfortunately, it didn't reach the bicameral conference committee report.

**[01:52:48.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002826)  
For various reasons.

**[01:52:50.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002827)  
But let me just talk about this NCR order 27.

**[01:52:55.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002828)  
One, it's very clear in Article 126 of the Labor Code that the court should not

**[01:53:04.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002829)  
and will not in any way issue a temporary restraining order.

**[01:53:10.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002830)  
And yet, dito po sa Pasig RTC, nakapag-issue ng TRO.

**[01:53:17.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002831)  
Yan ang sitwasyon.

**[01:53:21.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002832)  
And then, nung ma-issue po yung TRO, dahil temporary nga po,

**[01:53:24.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002833)  
sabi din po doon sa interview doon sa clerk of court,

**[01:53:28.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002834)  
kung wala hong mabibigay na bond na amounting to 10 billion pesos,

**[01:53:35.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002835)  
hindi magkakaroon ng preliminary injunction.

**[01:53:38.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002836)  
But unfortunately, ngayon, correct me if I'm wrong, again, just to put the record straight,

**[01:53:44.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002837)  
may preliminary injunction.

**[01:53:46.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002838)  
Okay.

**[01:53:47.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002839)  
Nagkaroon ng preliminary injunction, ang DOLE issued naman itong NCR 28, Wage Order 28.

**[01:53:59.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002840)  
Now, in effect, while NCR 27 remains under litigation.

**[01:54:07.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002841)  
So, yung question po dito, what is DOLE's legal position on the additional 25 pesos,

**[01:54:16.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002842)

**[01:54:22.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002843)  
Now, it's supposed to be scheduled on January of 2027, next year, no?

**[01:54:29.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002844)  
Kasi yun yung mga questions po eh.

**[01:54:32.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002845)  
Kung yung NCR Wage Order 27 ay ultimately ma-upheld,

**[01:54:40.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002846)  
yung tinatanong kayo ng Senator Bong,

**[01:54:42.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002847)  
will the 25 pesos, second tranche, remain?

**[01:54:46.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002848)  
Payable in addition to the 60 pesos rate presently implemented under itong bagong NCR Wage Order 28?

**[01:54:58.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002849)  
Or, does DOLE consider NCR 28 to have somehow superseded or otherwise affected NCR Wage Order 27?

**[01:55:12.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002850)  
Ang magandaw dito sa Senado, napag-uusapan ang gusto.

**[01:55:15.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002851)  
Hindi pa sigawan tayo dito, hindi pa taasa ng boses.

**[01:55:19.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002852)  
Mas gusto namin maintindihan yung ating posisyon dahil kahit magkaiba ho yung maging posisyon po natin,

**[01:55:26.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002853)  
we will respect each other's position.

**[01:55:29.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002854)  
Secretary Tolentino, please.

**[01:55:30.480] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002855)  
Salamat po. Medyo masalimut nga po yung katanungan yun at kung ano yung magiging resulta.

**[01:55:36.260] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002856)  
Kaya kanina pa ho, sinabi ko na yung fate, yung kahinat na ng Wage Order 27

**[01:55:43.560] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002857)  
ay ibinibigay na po natin sa husgado.

**[01:55:46.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002858)  
Kung sinabi po ng husgado, ganito, ganito, ganito, yun po yung ating susundin.

**[01:55:51.520] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002859)  
Dalawa po yung ating ginawa dito sa pag-issue ng Wage Order 28.

**[01:55:56.860] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002860)  
Ginalang po natin, nirespeto po natin, yumuko po tayo sa mandato ng husgado.

**[01:56:04.400] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002861)  
We respected the two RTCs, the two Supreme Court cases filed, and even the Supreme Court na pinakamataas na hukuman.

**[01:56:13.020] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002862)  
Na pending pa rin ngayon. Ginalang po natin sila.

**[01:56:16.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002863)  
Hinihintay natin kung...

**[01:56:18.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002864)  
Despite our disagreement.

**[01:56:19.420] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002865)  
Yes, opo.

**[01:56:20.360] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002866)  
Thank you.

**[01:56:21.040] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002867)  
Yung nakalagay sa Article 126.

**[01:56:25.640] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002868)  
Kasi ang sabi nila, pag wala daw pong ban, ay dapat ay hindi na dapat igalang.

**[01:56:31.920] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002869)  
Ang sa atin po dito, may mga desisyon ng Supreme Court, kahit po walang ban, dapat ang husgado rin na magsabi na walang ban, dapat ito ay patupad nyo na.

**[01:56:41.200] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002870)  
So balit hinintay po natin, hinintay po natin lahat, at ginalang natin.

**[01:56:47.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002871)  
So balit, ipinaglaban po natin.

**[01:56:50.380] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002872)  
Ipinaglalaban po natin, in fact po, Your Honor, sa October 2, ay deadline namin para sumagot po sa RTC na botas.

**[01:56:59.000] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002873)  
Ang sasagot po nito ay ang Office of Solicitor General.

**[01:57:02.720] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002874)  
Doon po sa Supreme Court, ang alam ko po ay humingi pa ng ilang days ang Office of the Solicitor General para tumugon.

**[01:57:10.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002875)  
Para mag-file ng comment.

**[01:57:12.440] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002876)  
Yung dalawang kaso po sa Court of Appeals ay hindi pa po pinapakomment ang Office of the Solicitor General.

**[01:57:21.300] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002877)  
Si RTC 152, dahil kami nag-file ng, pinagpatuloy po namin ipaglaban nito, nag-file kami ng motion for reconsideration,

may narapat po ng 152 nung nag-file sa...

**[01:57:39.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002879)  
Before you continue.

Before you continue, Secretary Tolentino, another former colleague of yours misses you, so he's here.

**[01:57:47.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002881)

**[01:57:52.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002882)  
Pwede ko kayong pumalakpak kung gusto niyong magkaroon ng pagbabago itong malaking binawas sa DOLE.

**[01:57:59.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002883)  
Yes, Secretary Tolentino, please continue.

**[01:58:01.940] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002884)  
Medyo nawala po ako dahil sa pag...

**[01:58:08.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002886)

**[01:58:10.180] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002887)  
Pagdating po ng paggalang sa pagdating kay Senator J.V., nakakalimutan ko na yung sasabihin ko.

**[01:58:15.660] DOLE Secretary Francis N. Tolentino** (asr-002888)

**[02:00:30.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002912)

**[02:01:02.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-002913)

**[02:47:33.420] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004704)

**[02:47:37.780] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004705)

**[02:47:42.580] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004706)

**[02:47:46.760] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004707)

**[02:47:49.960] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004708)

**[02:47:55.860] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004709)

**[02:47:58.300] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004710)

**[02:48:01.240] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004711)

**[02:48:03.420] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004712)

**[02:48:06.860] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004713)

**[02:48:10.740] DOLE BWC Director Alvin B. Curada** (asr-004714)  
just to cover po lahat ng small, medium, and large under inspection approach.
